## Hypothesis, implementation and evidence

- BRR_OBSERVER=1 requires AUDIO_OBSERVER=1 and defaults off. It records
  32 complete 16-byte BRR chunks, with raw inputs before range transformation
  and outputs after all four stores. Pause-only audio-brr exports 512 bytes.
  It does not change the decoder, ring PCM or AI submission policy.
- 48 linked hook pairs cover eight voices, slots 0/31 and completion wrap.
  Only owned trace bytes/counters and existing scratch registers change;
  HI/LO/CP0 survive. Hook overhead is 40 scalar instructions per chunk.
- 57,344 voice cases check arithmetic and trace fields; four mutations fail.
  Production parser: 186 cases plus 1,000 fuzz inputs. Recorder: 102 cases.
  Reboot/framebuffer: 12/5 cases; arena: 41 owners. Disabled CPU sections
  match R20; all 24 RSP sections remain identical. Arena gap remains 0xA38.
- Physical menu-mediated install/readback and input acknowledgements pass.
  At the same retired room boundary, 67,200 RGB pixels match coordinate8
  and 8,192 PCM bytes plus DSP state match R20, with zero differences.
- 32 real N64 records contain 128 half-scaled samples, all matching the
  unmodified compiled Snes9x 1.63 decode_brr method. Physical coverage is
  seven voices, filter 2 and three ranges; synthetic checks cover the rest.
  This verifies a bounded hardware decoder case, not full audio/DAC fidelity.
- No timing window was measured with these extra hooks. Their overhead must
  not be attributed to observer-free production code or a speed improvement.

# 2026-10-11 - Exact DSP pitch cache, physical preservation and BRR discrepancy (R19)

Source phase4/usb-hardware-loop:
84531758f4c70d7958d5267b740d4517f4a4c4f3 (parent d4cc32f).

[Curated aggregate evidence](evidence/pitch-cache-2026-10-11.json).
[Native history](CODEX_HARDWARE_HISTORY_2026-10-11.md).
[USB recipe and evidence limits](USB_HARDWARE_LOOP.md).

## Hypothesis, implementation and evidence

- DSP_PITCH_CACHE=1 is opt-in and defaults0. Both pitch-byte handlers update
  a raw14-bit cache after the original register store; invalid writes return
  before either store. No host-clock/DSP_SAMPLE-dependent conversion is cached.
- Actual sample conversion7->3 instructions, register writer15->26. Frequent
  writes can erase the saving; no universal performance claim. Raw reads,
  guest/APU cycle debit and memory-tag updates survive.
- Linked tests:131072 writes,524288 cached loads,65536 uncached conversions,
  128 invalid writes,80 ordinary writes,2048 interleaved/read cases and16
  production APU caller cases. A high-mask mutation is rejected.
- Disabled CPU text/rodata/data is byte-identical to selected coordinate8;
  all24 RSP text/data sections match.41-owner arena and runtime bounds PASS;
  12 reboot/5 frame,16 observer and102 recorder cases PASS.
- Physical input replay at matching retired boundaries compares4 images:
  268800 pixels,0 differences. Three rings:24576 PCM bytes,0 differences,
  same ring heads, enabled voices and128 DSP registers. This preserves old
  PCM; it does not prove SNES sound fidelity.
- Three quiet USB-only20s captures have complete checksum-valid traces,
  overflow0, settings precision20/skip0/APU21/audio4/layers0. Title first300
  cadence53.645934->55.009215 (+2.541%), room59.515700->59.854494 (+0.569%).
  Rain32.185454->32.233815 (+0.150%) is not claimed as a win from one window.
- Quiet audio production versus nominal AI: title94.45%,room99.54%,rain53.52%.
  In rain361/361 submits observe idle,FULL0. Low guest progress causes a
  concrete undersupply mechanism. Initial paused drain/endpoint overhead
  remain; these are not exact lost-sample or DAC-waveform counts.

## Discovery and next condition

A synthetic BRR audit uses7168 blocks, all four filters/ranges and selected
previous-sample states. Its arithmetic oracle matches the unmodified compiled
Snes9x1.63 decode_brr method in all7168 cases; fetched source matches the local
reference after newline normalization. Current sodium64 differs in5660 blocks
(321 filter0,1730 filter1,1778 filter2,1831 filter3): invalid ranges13-15,
signed filter rounding and saturation/wrapping. No commercial audio is used.
This is a specific decoder discrepancy, not an audible-defect attribution.

Next: isolate a mathematically faithful BRR correction with an independent
reference and mutation checks, then measure on N64 without changing image.
Pitch cache remains available to offset CPU work; whole audio still lacks
qualified echo/master volume/interpolation/release/PMON/mixing. Rain still
requires renderer work. No Road1.0 completion or constant60 presentation.

## Recorder and control results

- A14's extra files were exclusive menu reservations, not completed captures.

  up to16 files; no OK/S64D complete means no measurement.
- Preserve first SC64/PI error, DATA0 and error phase; initialize/check card
  state and serialize PI/register ownership. Bounded data sectors, incomplete
  header, body readback, final complete header/readback and no slot wrap remain.
  Two successive physical SD captures with valid checksums resumed gameplay.
  Immutable USB snapshot and notice-expiry wrap fixes are now included.
- USB responds while N64 is on. Menu-mediated permanent file replacement is
  verified; direct PC SD ownership while console owns it is still excluded.
- Launch selects the ROM through the real INI/emulator/save/pool loader and
  offset0x104000, not execution of the empty emulator alone.
- Missing rom:/cursorsound.wav64 was mismatched menu DFS/assets. FatFs80386
  headers must match linked libdragon55aac1355773169d87e45424b772faf0dfb9432a
  across all consumers. f_rename replaces unsupported POSIX rename.
- Bootloader return preserves H/V timing and waits for VI_CURRENT0 before
  stopping output. TV type is captured from DMEM+9 before RSP overwrites it;
  RDRAM0x300 is not the modern N64 TV-type source. The user confirmed SMW→menu
  visible over USB without Reset. Nonresponsive code may still need power off.
- Menu and emulator expose cooperative launch/install, retired pause/step,
  acknowledged controller injection, capture/frame/SAV/profile/sections and
  return. USB framebuffer precedes VI/analog/HDMI/TV; no physical power control.
- Pool cursor scans existing names once per batch:112 attempts for96 old+16 new;
  26 cases and native launch30.328s, versus previous216s. Reservations stay
  distinct from captures and are not silently deleted/reused.

## Retained renderer work and negative results

Sparse dirty-VRAM copies provide only a small room gain; they are not the main
bottleneck. Atomic SP SIG4–6 residency identifies Math/Main, including DMA/
resident helpers, and replaces unsafe non-atomic bank/PC attribution.

Retained: guarded128-pixel Math batches; compact eligibility tags; effective-mask
scanning; exact unary TLUT classes for fixed/absent Sub; full-row Math DMA with
VRAM/OAM restoration; narrowly admitted RDP half arithmetic; exact fixed-window
Math; bounded small-fixed RDP arithmetic and batching; coordinate atlas8 with
legacy visible-Sub fallback, version/palette/source fences and mask/scissor
restoration. Original packed RGB555 semantics and forbidden-class fallbacks
remain required. Most experimental flags are opt-in; unflagged make is not
the selected native configuration.

Rejected: general full RDP add/sub and half-then-double (native synthetic
mismatches); atlas32 (slower); generic atlas8 (story gain but rain-7.08%);
flat COLOR-window TLUT (no measurable gain); coordinate atlas4 (story-0.72%).
The initial flat-window oracle inverted selectors1/2; original compiled Setup
and independent primary clip semantics caught it before hardware. Corrected
truth tests do not turn its near-zero timing result into a gain.

# 2026-10-04 - A14 with combined renderer and continuous captures

The user chose one paired menu/emulator installation with repeated captures:
N64 Start -> approximately 20 s -> verified unique SAV -> OK -> keep playing
and rearm without reset. A13+A+B are combined. The menu reserves 16 files per
game session (512 KiB SD); unused reservations are not measured scenes.

Source: phase4/gate-c-a14-combined at 018630ffb8944f22dcdfd3dcafb427a7b2e4cd42.
Prior combined control 328b65a passed Build 37235470435 and Gate 37235470458.
New source passed Build and paired menu. The user explicitly requested immediate delivery
and deferred remaining checks to the next prompt; final Gate was still running.
Normal whole-frame images and original v2/v4 recorder steps had passed, but
armed image qualification/artifact verification remain pending for this package. Exact candidate workflows:
Build 37238884074, Gate 37238884032 and paired menu 37238884035.

S64D v5 retains v4 geometry and adds CON5 timestamps/context. Measurements seal
at the timer IRQ cutoff; guest SRAM and retired renderer context come from a
separately timestamped natural frame boundary. Normal completion never forces
RSP HALT/DP FREEZE. The O64 bridge owns a private stack and preserves ordinary
GPR64, HI/LO, Status/EXL and queue-index sp. SD work and OK display are outside
the retained interval. Later stalls remain terminal, with their own context
and without inflating the sealed measurement denominator.

The paired menu pins e28c26e1aeae3c18851fc24118080e23e5a86a9f. It closes and syncs
exclusive FA_CREATE_NEW reservations, checks prior writeback, disables automatic
writeback via load_save(NULL), imports ordinary guest SRAM read-only and passes
fragmented sector lists, CSD/CID and data bounds in a checksummed S64C descriptor
at cart offsets 0x102000..0x104000. Sodium writes only these bounded, globally
disjoint data sectors: incomplete header first, each body sector read back,
then complete header verified before OK. Slots never wrap, including partial
writes. Soft restart skips nonzero diagnostic regions. Exhaustion or failure
blocks rearm while gameplay can continue. Failure retains the diagnostic 24 KiB;
the guest 8 KiB remains mutable. No retry, full immutable snapshot or USB sink
is implemented.

Qualification: 79 compiled cases, including 36 captures across 12 sessions, 8 failure/
exhaustion cases, 24 actual O64 bridges, 4 invalid handoffs, 4 post-seal faults,
2 IRQ/frame races and legacy behavior. The actual C SD algorithm passed 268 host
cases; the real menu helper passed 26. Linux ASan/UBSan passed. Hardware boundaries
are mocked, so these tests do not establish physical persistence, latency,
audio/gameplay resumption or FPS. Live DMEM bank/stage/PPU candidates retain
the existing hardware-access and attribution limits.

Work removed: A core 67/73 -> 22/26 issued instructions per 8 active add/sub pixels;
B removes 250,880 DMA bytes per 224-row general Sub section while retaining Sub
drawing/SyncFull and selecting Main's separate Z owner. Counts are not native
cycles/FPS; net physical gain is unmeasured. No constant 60 FPS claim is made.

Deliverable: Sodium64-A14-capturas-continuas.zip (1789095 bytes;
SHA256 687faf1f856369eb0ef50b1e63d0d9e4b6e3084b6d442f36406a8d52f817af7c; 49 entries). One empty trace.z64, paired menu, one INI, exact CPU/RSP
symbols/maps, decoder v5, available Build/menu proof metadata, integration/source pins and
licenses. ROMs, SAVs and private frames/dumps are excluded. The SD is unchanged.
Instructions require one installation with menu/INI backups and preserve A13
and old SAVs. Menu assets are unchanged from the pinned upstream; identity
with the installed customized UI is unverified.

Future USB iteration needs acknowledged safe upload/reboot, frame-indexed input
replay, capture receipt and visual/audio export. COM3 was not opened; firmware
is unchanged. Continuous v5 writes directly to SD, so generic USB SRAM-memory
download does not retrieve this new sink's files.

Master at 7cc8fac, gameplay a4 at 7381c17 and A13 at b420614 remain unchanged. No PR,
merge, release or hardware acceptance occurred. Next: finish deferred final Gate verification and paired physical A14 trials
on the same SMW/DKC1/ALttP scenes without INI swaps or reset between captures.

## 2026-10-04 — Physical A11/A13 batch analyzed; two independent experiments fully qualified

- User confirmed A13 DKC/ALttP 000002/000003/000004 correspond to scenes 1/2/3; A11 uses 000001/000002/000003. Thirteen complete v4 captures pass checksum, waits/counters, full retention and zero-overflow checks, with MAX, frameskip 0, full APU clock 21 and audio ON. The named A11 ALttP intro save is absent. Additional A13 000001 files have no diagnostic header and a zero diagnostic region; they are launch reservations, not fourth captured scenes. Ordinary guest-save areas were preserved. All commercial ROM/SAV/raw analyses/state/footage/hash data remain private.
- Reliable evidence: Count completed boundaries, explicit CPU RSP/VI waits and CPU EPCs against exact A11/A13 CI ELF/map. These are not presentation FPS or exclusive RSP durations; CPU prepares N+1 during RSP N. Unsupported live DMEM bank/stage/PPU fields remain unvalidated candidates. Current diagnostic/causal/architecture docs now correct the former epoch-bookend attribution claim. Historical checkpoints are retained.
- Sustained endpoint windows after 5 s through capture end (~20 s): DKC intro 27.28→28.24 completed boundaries/s, stage 1 8.65→19.00; ALttP room 19.26→24.50, exterior 17.22→18.57. A13 mean explicit CPU RSP waits are 24.30, 37.48, 28.37 and 40.10 ms respectively. SMW's later window is 59.52→59.50/s with almost zero RSP wait; its worst iris boundaries still approach 39 ms. Captures are matched by scene, not synchronized to identical guest frames.
- DKC developer-logo capture changes bottleneck: initial RSP waiting, then CPU/APU compiler/emitter-heavy work with almost zero RSP wait (A13 ~12.2–17.2 s), then RSP waiting again. Do not present its global average as a same-state speedup. Existing EPCs motivate generic APU compiler/invalidation/cache investigation but do not prove why repeated compilation occurs; no speculative APU semantic patch was applied.
- Experiment A: `phase4/gate-c-packed-math-experiment`, `3a0c7fc357cb0e8df5eb8f6aaa0b221c8b32df60`, based on A13 b420614. Exact packed RGB555 arithmetic preserves clipping, winner/OBJ eligibility, Sub presence, fixed fallback and HALF rules. Actual compiled code passes 159,744 arithmetic pixels plus 81,920 selection pixels against an independent per-channel oracle. Core issues per eight pixels reduce 67/73→22/26 for add/subtract; these are instruction counts, not native timing. First CI exposed missing MTC2 support in an old scalar address VM; support was added without skipping its 1,986 address/consumer assertions. Final Build `37226915786` and full Gate `37226915788` pass at exact head.
- Experiment B: `phase4/gate-c-sub-presence-dataflow`, `76f3f935e60f774cbfca6fd46d8180a091663c97`, independently based on A13. Sub Z writes to presence directly; after the existing Sub SyncFull fence, Main selects winner, including TS=0. Source colors, masks, source renderer, required retirement, raw/direct policies and arithmetic remain intact. Removes 250,880 two-way snapshot DMA bytes per full-general 224-row section and the snapshot overlay trip. Independent compiled review passes 5,816 transitions, 4,362 fences, six raw/direct bypasses and 1,986 alignment/consumer cases. Final Build `37227084880` and full Gate `37227084878` pass.
- For EACH candidate: all 94 normal complete images match 5,390,336 pixels; all 12 armed v4 images match 688,128 pixels; compact guards pass, no framebuffer seeding or guest/diagnostic-state writes. Original manual/v4 recorders complete ~20 seconds, v4 whole retention and overflow zero, PI cartridge payload unchanged. ## 2026-10-04 UTC — A12 OBJ regression repaired; full pixel requalification pending

**IMPLEMENTED / VALIDATED (compiled):** the A12 graphics regression has a concrete source cause and a bounded repair. Runtime candidate `7381c1779597c2c32c4ef13e262418ca4e39cee9` is now the head of `phase4/gate-c-a4-gameplay-capture`; master remains unchanged. In both `src/rsp_main.S` and `src/rsp_mode7.S`, A12's invisible OBJ-row cull could branch directly into `hcomp_obj_row_done`. That continuation was written for a row that had actually traversed all X tiles: it rewound X by `v0` and character index by the row width before advancing to the next character row. A culled row had done neither traversal, so the same rewind displaced the next visible tile row. The repair distinguishes the two states using the existing `s0` row counter: only a fully drawn row performs the X/character-column rewind; both paths normalize `s0`, advance one character row and advance Y. This preserves culling rather than disabling the optimization.

**TEST GAP REPAIRED:** the prior A12 test exercised `hcomp_obj_row_clip` only until either its return or entry into `hcomp_obj_row_done`; it therefore proved the clipping predicate but never executed the skipped-row continuation. `scripts/test_renderer_batching.py` now executes actual skipped-row -> next-visible-row progression plus drawn-row continuation in both regular and Mode7 RSP images, across 8/16/32/64-pixel OBJ widths and horizontal/vertical traversal directions. Build and Validate run 37179331942 compiled successfully; its normal compiled suite passed `RENDERER_BATCHING` with 8,190 row-decision cases plus 48 new OBJ-progression cases, and normal/profile/native diagnostic/native trace builds plus resident ABI/runtime checks succeeded. The emulator smoke child job was still running at this checkpoint; it is not native-performance authority.

Source audit has found a concrete A12 OBJ row-culling regression in both regular and Mode7 variants. The new cull branches to hcomp_obj_row_done, which assumes that a full X tile row already advanced the X coordinate and character number. A skipped row has performed neither advance, so that continuation incorrectly subtracts one row's X width and tile count before the next visible row. This is independently reproducible from compiled state transitions; the previous helper-only clipping tests missed the continuation. Add a dedicated skipped-row continuation that retains X and character-column ownership, then test multirow progression and full-image original fixtures. This is a plausible source of the newly reported sprite glitches, not proof that every unpictured commercial glitch has the same cause.

No hardware ceiling is demonstrated. A12 qualification covered85 original images but not this multirow-cull progression. Do not claim those passes prove all-game fidelity. Current runtime branch `phase4/gate-c-a4-gameplay-capture` is `bacc62315087eb135d6cf5f60e65e083af96719b`. This supersedes the earlier work-in-progress recorder/ROM-alias notes. Runtime and menu helper semantics were finalized at `b9f1163d5940ea5c44395d436cee2d15407568df`; subsequent commits repair only the armed-image harness. Master remains `7cc8facfe8643fb85888f301f79995575830521d`. No PR, merge or release was made.

The v4 native recorder preserves a complete 20-second manual N64 START capture within the existing 24 KiB budget. It appends every completed-boundary Count interval and explicit CPU RSP/VI waits; records CPU domain counts and RSP source/control, composition, arithmetic, DMA/RDP-wait stages on every timer IRQ; and groups observations at 125 ms. Retained EPC sampling is reduced to make room for complete boundary timing. RSP overlay classification uses a bookended bank/epoch protocol; inconsistent, pending, invalid or excessively delayed reads are unknown, never guessed. Resident source/control and resident wait PCs are classified separately from the loaded overlay. First partial arm interval is excluded. Completed boundaries are not exact presentation FPS, overlapping CPU/RSP work is not additive, and latched RDP busy bits are not exact utilization.

One diagnostic emulator works with repeated launches of the SAME personal ROM. A narrowly scoped opt-in SummerCart64 menu patch reserves `ROMstem-diag-v4-bacc623-NNNNNN.sav` with exclusive creation, choosing the first unused identifier at launch. It imports at most the first 8 KiB of the ordinary save read-only and prepares a separate 32 KiB diagnostic destination. Previous captures and ordinary progress are preserved; session progress belongs to that capture. Failed or unfinished reservations are skipped on later launches. One capture per launch, then reset to the menu and reload. No ROM copies/aliases, in-emulator game browser, firmware flashing or hot switching. Games requiring more than 8 KiB SRAM refuse capture before recorder SRAM writes. Fatal file/read/sync/close failures reject launch. Menu source is pinned to N64FlashcartMenu `e28c26e1aeae3c18851fc24118080e23e5a86a9f`; SD writeback uses the existing sector driver.

Validation: [Gate CI 37166088720](https://github.com/ironangelo/sodium64/actions/runs/37166088720) and [Build CI 37166088723](https://github.com/ironangelo/sodium64/actions/runs/37166088723) succeeded at bacc623; [Menu CI 37165369649](https://github.com/ironangelo/sodium64/actions/runs/37165369649) succeeded at b9f1163. The compiled observer passed 227 cases and manual arming 16. Synthetic whole capture retained 20.031459925 seconds, 1,198 complete intervals, 159 observations and 224 retained EPC samples without overflow; cart PI payload matched all 32,768 bytes. Eight naturally executed guest image oracles with the recorder armed passed all 458,752 checked pixels, including direct/general, iris rows and Mode7. The normal binary is byte-identical to qualified A11 (SHA256 `12347820b723c21b8ca87f2ef0b2794cfb10dd875719d25bfac9da320813a942`). Previous full A11 image qualification remains applicable by byte identity; do not claim all 84 images were rerun this turn. Host menu helper passed repeated same-ROM naming, save preservation and failure-path tests with sanitizers.

The measured observer body fraction was about 0.1125% in the synthetic emulator run only; cache, bus and bank-tag effects are not fully measured. This is not evidence of real-console FPS or negligible hardware perturbation. Real SummerCart SD writeback/reboot behavior and the new recorder on commercial gameplay still require the user's first hardware capture.

Prepared empty package `sodium64-diagnostic-v4-bacc623.zip` includes the qualified emulator, final corrected menu, INI example, Spanish instructions, matching reader/ELF/maps, source/patch/license and synthetic qualification. It contains no games, real SAVs or footage. Package SHA256 `7f4c7d98233bccb9d496c6d7eed03220f944330e5a4524f4de9810dd1199fd27`; native trace binary SHA256 `5dfc3efa3dfa783726abe64cdd8637b0547d12828489144739d80e070d738971`; menu SHA256 `0220b93c1adb5b2577c3edd60e63f60dd84c8092284e1b4e24ac41bb556754a6`. Earlier menu artifacts with the incorrect tag terminator are not delivered.

Latest user asks whether repeating the SMW intro now determines the specific drop more certainly. Answer: yes, individual timing/waits plus source-backed sampled work distinguish bottlenecks better than busy-only profiling, but the cause of expensive work still requires a controlled change/repeat and output preservation. ## 2026-10-03 UTC — Immediate recommendation: bounded cross-game capture before committing to a renderer rewrite

The user asks sincerely which investment is better: reusable multi-game 20-second diagnostics and cause-based improvements, or a new renderer architecture. Recommendation is the first as a bounded evidence gate, not an endless case-by-case campaign: one diagnostic emulator, a small representative SMW/ALttP/DKC1 batch, then a single combined analysis and a decision on generic fixes versus the renderer experiment. Current internal hardware evidence covers SMW only; ALttP/DKC1 have user-reported fidelity/performance observations. The streamed renderer remains a justified candidate, not the selected expensive implementation.

Updated both planning documents so their sequencing agrees. Reuse the existing recorder; add only measurements needed to distinguish source/composition work, CPU/audio and ownership/transfer waits. Capture settings parity, full audio/APU and no skips, valid whole interval and observer effect, exact source/ELF/map and loader/SRAM admission. Compare a heavy and faster interval where practical. Do not present raw busy percentages as a proven cause.

Decision rule: a repeated avoidable invalidation/upload/wait/state transition calls for one generic fix tested across the same batch; a measured deficit in necessary complete renderer work calls for the bounded architecture prototype; unresolved attribution calls for one discriminating measurement, not a growing scene list. New saves should support ranking shared mechanisms rather than title-specific admission or hope that unrelated patches generalize. SMW progress is useful but cannot establish wider performance; it is also not treated as a trivial PPU workload merely because it is familiar.

Primary transport review: upstream N64FlashcartMenu selects sodium64.z64 for SNES with SRAM256Kbit by default; per-emulator overrides exist. cart_load.c chooses the save path from the loaded game's path, and sc64.c enables writeback to that file's sectors. Current Sodium/native_diag writes the cart SRAM payload and finishes at a saved-capture screen; it does not create named SD files or provide an in-emulator ROM browser. A single SD test session via the flashcart menu between games is a feasible design; hot switching inside Sodium is a separate feature.

Diagnostic/game/build save namespaces must be explicit and verified before delivery. Minimal implementation can use local diagnostic-named game copies/aliases and a single emulator so launcher-derived SAVs stay separate; output suffixes independent of the launch filename require additional launcher/SD integration. Verify preservation of ordinary game saves, bounded guest SRAM admission and complete saved payload. Upstream docs/source are not proof of the user's installed version. No commercial game bytes or saves enter public source/CI.

This turn provides a decision and documentation, not a built generic recorder or a measured cross-game speedup. Runtime baseline remains A11 ac534849002a04d342dd566a6f96ae1707680387; no source runtime change, PR, merge, release or master mutation. Only the three curated documentation paths change.

## 2026-10-03 UTC — Road-aligned architecture decision: test the complete renderer dataflow

The user rejects an open-ended scene-by-scene fast-admission campaign and asks how to reach Road 1.0 from current fidelity/performance. This is steering of the existing goal. The earlier causal recorder proposal is useful but insufficient as the primary route; Road explicitly warns against hundreds of small builds without measured movement and instrumentation becoming the project.

Added docs/GATE_C_RENDERER_ARCHITECTURE_PLAN.md and marked its priority in the causal profiling plan. Next proposed milestone is a bounded general renderer architecture experiment, not another speculative wrapper or full recorder implementation by itself. A11 remains the qualified regression/reference baseline, with runtime source unchanged at ac534849002a04d342dd566a6f96ae1707680387. Established pixel fidelity is a requirement; A11 is not assumed to be an exhaustive SNES oracle.

Preferred candidate is streamed block resolution: bring Main/Sub candidates, layer/OBJ eligibility, priorities and window state together in owned RSP working storage, apply the complete exact operator family and commit final pixels per block. Parameterize kernels by hardware state, separate dependency epochs and reuse valid geometry/tile/palette work. Avoid intermediate images/copies/readbacks that exist only to recover already-known metadata. Preserve all real raster boundaries and guest-visible events; per-layer windows can expose lower layers, so masking a flattened image is invalid.

This is a hypothesis requiring complete-cost comparison. Current general arithmetic is already vectorized; enabling SIMD is not new. Source-derived current composition traffic alone reaches 824,320 bytes over 224 fully general nonblank rows, excluding source/RDP/texture traffic, but that is not a measured bandwidth/FPS explanation. A replacement may lose RDP's accelerated source production and add expensive RSP winner/decode work. Compare fused RSP production/resolution with retained RDP source production plus streamed RSP resolve before selecting the complete path. Fixed data/code workspace ownership and lifetime dependencies may require a deliberate renderer-internal layout change.

Stages: complete independently checked resolution kernel; add representative source production/raster changes and include all costs; evaluate both general arithmetic and short-epoch direct workloads; integrate at one renderer boundary with full CPU/APU/DSP and output comparison; validate the same engine across the fixed game corpus. Kernel-only speed is a lower bound, not emulator FPS. Minimal measurement serves this architecture decision; full 20-second recorder work is not a prerequisite.

Acceptance requires exact arithmetic/window/priority/rounding behavior and measured complete-cost reduction that closes a defined deadline deficit, followed by integrated native cadence with full audio and no skipping. If the candidate still misses budget or loses output correctness, identify the failing compute/source/transfer dependency and revise the design before a long migration. Tiny gains that leave the complete path far outside budget do not justify another unranked build sequence.

No claim of global 60 FPS or a demonstrated N64 hardware limit. SMW live iris samples mostly use direct composition; ALttP/DKC1 internal routes remain uncaptured. The architecture experiment must include direct fragmentation and general composition, while CPU/audio and future coprocessor blockers retain separate evidence requirements. Normal runtime, master, existing user artifacts and privacy boundaries are unchanged. No PR/merge/release; only three curated documentation paths are updated.

## 2026-10-03 UTC — ALttP/DKC1 observations broaden the performance blocker; causal profiling design

The user tested the empty normal A11 with ALttP and DKC1. Reported visual fidelity is excellent in the inspected scenes and must be preserved. Reported performance is about 20 FPS in several A11 Medium/Max scenes that original Sodium64 renders at about 60 FPS in Medium with visual glitches. ALttP: title, new-game telepathy/dark house, night/rain exterior. DKC1: developer screen, animated Cranky/DK intro and first-stage gradient-horizon gameplay; static logo and parts of the second rainy stage are faster. These are user observations, not decoded captures of those games; settings parity and exact routes are not yet established. Do not infer that rain, gradients or sprite count are proven causes.

Source review confirms raw Main-only, exact direct and general HCOMP policies. General composition uses <=8-row bands, Sub/Main/provenance/presence handling and vector arithmetic. Source-derived per fully general nonblank 256-pixel row: 8 batches x (4 input + 1 output) x 64 bytes = 40 arithmetic DMA operations / 2,560 bytes, plus 1,120 bytes for the two-way 560-byte Sub/provenance snapshot. At 224 general rows this accounts for 824,320 bytes excluding texture/overlay DMA and RDP traffic. Masks are rebuilt across 256 horizontal entries per general band. These are structural work counts, not measured cycles or bandwidth/FPS claims. TILE_TABLE is reused by the renderer and snapshot, so unchanged window registers alone do not justify mask reuse without an ownership redesign.

The existing SMW capture constrains the common-cause hypothesis: all 21 live observations in the iris-heavy interval and all 21 in the strongest later microdip report direct policy2. Whole capture: 394 direct observations and 2 raw. Live samples are non-atomic, not an exhaustive band census, but they do not support explaining the known SMW iris as general arithmetic. Distinguish fragmented direct rendering from expensive general composition and CPU/audio preparation.

Added docs/GATE_C_CAUSAL_PROFILING_PLAN.md: proposed hybrid measurement with frame-boundary Count timing, explicit CPU waits, classified CPU sampling, validated active-bank RSP stage/wait observations, DP counter deltas and actual rendering-work counts/rejection categories. Correlate logical frames across the pipelined CPU/RSP queues; do not sum concurrent subsystem percentages. Use one controlled, output-preserving experiment after the leading cost is measured. Original synthetic recipes isolate captured conditions; real-game scenes remain the performance authority.

Retention is explicitly budgeted against the recorder's 24 KiB free after the first 8 KiB guest reservation: 512-byte header + 1,280 x 12-byte compact frames + 20 x 96-byte interval summaries + 2 x 48 x 64-byte detail pools = 23,936 bytes, 640 spare. This is a verified capacity proposal, not a final implemented schema. Preserve whole-interval compact data, bounded append-only pools, flags for exhaustion/quantization/partial tails and measured observer effect. A generic diagnostic build must validate each loader/SRAM configuration; current SMW-specific <=8 KiB assumptions cannot silently be applied to all games.

The user asks why separate fast/general routes exist. Explanation: fast routes replace mathematically equivalent special cases with less work; dispatch is based on graphics state, not recognized game scenes. General composition handles operations that those shortcuts cannot calculate. A complete faster algorithm remains a valid goal; routing unsupported states through a shortcut would lose fidelity. Measure conservative admission, repeated work and general-algorithm cost rather than accepting a permanently slow fallback or claiming a hardware ceiling.

No runtime patch, new emulator build, ROM packaging, PR, merge, release or master mutation in this design checkpoint. Runtime stays ac534849002a04d342dd566a6f96ae1707680387. Only the two curated documentation paths enter continuity; commercial ROMs/SAVs/video/extracted state and guest hashes remain private.

The A11 iris window averages 38.58 completed FPS; CPU waits for RSP in 31.9% of occupancy samples, SP is running in 94.7%, DP pipe busy is sampled in 93.1%, and DP command busy in 41.9%. The strongest later microdip window averages 56.31 completed FPS with no sampled CPU RSP wait, SP running 53.9%, DP pipe busy 50.8%, and DP command busy 40.0%. That subset remains part of CPU-other and must not be added as a separate exclusive bucket. CPU/APU/DSP/PPU/DMA are still mixed inside CPU-other; sparse retained EPCs do not identify an authoritative per-frame culprit. Brief unsampled events and observer perturbation remain limitations.

Next proposal, not yet implemented: bounded causal profiling by logical frame, including CPU preparation/work domains and explicit waits; active RSP bank/stage with drawing versus RDP/DMA stalls; validated RDP counter deltas and submission/fence/transfer counts correlated with the same frame and raster-section count. Keep the whole 20-second interval as compact frame summaries, with separately bounded detailed retention for the iris and later microdips, and qualify/report observer cost. Then test a controlled alternative against the measured dominant cost before another speculative batch. No need for a repeat of the same A11 SAV at this point. Full clocks, audio, raster precision and visual fidelity remain requirements; constant 60 FPS is still unsolved.

The final telemetry window shows CPU RSP-wait100%, SP running100%, DP command+pipe busy100%, TMEM busy0%, while DP clock/command/pipe cycle counters continue advancing. This is a genuine RDP command/pipeline deadlock, not a stale FPS overlay, CPU emulator loop, menu bug or stopped RSP.

**FREEZE COMMAND STREAM / SUPPORTED CAUSE:** captured DMEM `0xCA8..0xCCF` is the ordinary `rdp_tile` list: SetTextureImage -> **TileSync** -> LoadBlock -> TextureRectangle. The immediately preceding list at `0xC98..0xCA7` is TileSync -> SetScissor; Nintendo documents that Scissor is one of the few attributes not requiring PipeSync, while texture loads after primitive rendering require **LoadSync** and the standard texture-load macros place LoadSync before LoadBlock. Sodium64 instead emits TileSync at that position. The current list was accepted (no START/END pending latch) but could not consume even its first word while command/pipeline DMA remained permanently busy. The strongest supported root cause is therefore an invalid/under-synchronized RDP texture-load stream that emulators tolerate but real silicon can deadlock. This is now an ARCHITECTURE/PROTOCOL bug, not merely the sender's DMEM-ownership wait. The post-reset libdragon screen reporting “RDP CRASHED / hardware bug” with freeze+pipe+DMA+START+END is consistent aftermath of the same wedged RDP, not a second independent failure.

**FPS / MEASURED:** diagnostic observer body cost is only0.156%, so the recorder does not explain the slowdown. Completed-frame cadence by successive ~1s windows was52.57,41.54,23.80,24.79,23.78,24.79,24.79,40.41,34.54,21.80,9.92,0.00 FPS. In the stable ~24FPS windows CPU samples spend76–88% in `rsp_wait` while SP running is100%; DP command-busy is only~13–16%. Therefore the pre-freeze throughput ceiling is the **RSP renderer path**, not R4300 S-CPU/APU work and not ordinary RDP raster throughput.

**FPS / SUPPORTED CAUSE:** the current full-height HCOMP path admits <=8-row bands and, whenever color math is active, runs a software Main/Sub compositor on the RSP. The captured stalled band has `CGADSUB=0x20` (backdrop math only), `CGWSEL=0x12`, HCOMP band y125 / rows1, yet this disables the raw/no-op fast path and enters the general compositor. For each compact 8-row band, the math bank builds256 scalar window-mask entries and, per row, executes8 batches; each batch performs4x64-byte input DMAs + one64-byte output DMA plus vectorized three-channel arithmetic. That is40 DMA submissions/row,320/band and up to8,960 math-path DMA submissions across a224-line frame before ordinary renderer/HCOMP phase work. This architecture is the supported source of the large FPS regression and is consistent with the measured100% SP occupancy / CPU RSP waits. Exact intra-RSP percentage is not sampled, so retain this as SUPPORTED INTERPRETATION rather than a per-function measured share.

**NEXT / PLANNED HARDWARE PACKAGE:** preserve causal isolation instead of folding freeze+performance into one opaque candidate. Prepare two same-session private diagnostic ROMs from the exact measured base. **A / RDP-SYNC CONTROL:** HCOMP and all performance code byte-identical to2b4; audit every RDP LoadBlock path (palette, regular tile, Mode7) and use the documented LoadSync/required ordering while retaining TileSync only for tile-descriptor hazards and preserving the proven DMEM ownership sender. Expand deterministic command-stream/protocol controls, full-image fixtures and build/ABI checks. Native acceptance: the same SMW reaches the20s TIME LIMIT rather than FRAME STALL, iris/demo progresses, and returned SRAM shows no wedged CURRENT/END. Do not interpret its FPS as improved. **B / HCOMP FAST1:** build only after A is qualified locally; retain A's RDP fix and add exactly one semantic performance change: a specialized path for the captured backdrop-only/fixed-color color-math family, with the existing general HCOMP path as oracle/fallback. Kill criterion: if a demonstrably large amount of HCOMP work is bypassed yet hardware cadence moves only marginally, stop micro-optimizing and re-evaluate compositor architecture rather than repeating SNES64-style +1FPS iteration. ## Shared event-arena repair — authorized / resource audit checkpoint (2026-10-01 Chile)

Iron explicitly requests the general event-arena fix in both phase4/gate-c-smw-exploration and phase4/gate-c-stage2-color-diag. Both currently780645a909747846ded7a27d80b504401091d840; master7cc8facf unchanged. Audit confirms Q1 needs0x18000 bytes, endsDAE00; originalQ2 D7000..EF000 collides with Q1 and live compact E2000/E4000/E6000 surfaces. The entire free gap cannot accommodate two complete queues AND all simultaneous bounded surfaces/base/sideband/raw shadows; simply shifting Q2 upward there is not a fix.

**Chosen bounded design for implementation/qualification:** preserve original0x6000-record/4-byte event capacity, originalQ1/base/sideband/compact surfaces/framebuffer/cache/JIT addresses and all RSP executable/ABI geometry. Relocate Q2 to physical3E8000..400000, within base4MiB RDRAM, reserving the final12 existing8KiB ROM paging-cache slots. ROMs are paged from cart (not loaded whole); therefore this changes resident ROM cache slots256→244, not supported cart-ROM byte size, and does not require Expansion Pak. Implement explicit244-slot wrap in tlbl_rom and check cache end against Q2 start. Keep ROM mapping/addrs/entries256-entry tables allocated, stale upperentries unused. Tradeoff:96KiB less resident ROM cache, potential additional PI misses on working sets above244pages; no claim of unchanged broad game performance. Alternatives rejected for this bounded repair: reduce admitted event capacity, borrow displayed framebuffer/overscan storage, shrink APUJIT/tile caches, place buffers beyond4MiB, or move only Q2 within the insufficient lower gap.

Before transferring to diagnostic branch: source/ELF full owner byte-range checks includingQ1/Q2/surfaces and actualROM-cache end; paging wrap/eviction model and native instruction coverage; exact normal/HW_PROFILE/color builds; unchanged allRSPtext/data and CPU scope outside addresses/ROM-wrap; originalvisual/mixed continuous actualPI traces and fresh frozenA/B oracle; independent downloaded artifact/hash/decode. Add targeted highpalette-pressure execution if feasible; label any unexecuted highpressure contract explicitly. Existing native hue/pixeladvance result and deliveredoriginalROMs remain preserved. No repair published/PASS yet; this checkpoint records resource decision before implementation.

**CONFIRMED NEW AUDIT DEFECT — event arena units and overlap.** src/ppu.S compares hcomp_cgram_event_count with HCOMP_CGRAM_EVENT_CAPACITY0x6000 and increments the counter by1 while advancing each stored event pointer by4. Capacity therefore means24576 four-byte records (98304bytes/0x18000), as the historical consumer contract explicitly states. Current Q1=C2E00 → DAE00 at declared capacity; Q2=D7000 → EF000. Q1/Q2 overlap0x3E00 bytes; even the documented legal worst-case20780 records makes Q1 endD72B0,704bytes into Q2. Q2 reaches compact SubZ atE0000 after9216 records, MainZ E2000 after11264, SubColor E4000 after13312 and preserved TSZ E6000 after15360, all below the admitted24576 and documented20780 maxima. The main/sub layout proof instead uses q+capacity (not q+4*capacity), incorrectly claiming Q2 endsDD000 and these surfaces are in a free gap. Repair investigation doc repeats the byte-sized ranges. check_runtime_arena validates linked sections versus the overall arena only; it cannot detect these internal overlaps. Direct calls to existing contract helpers reject current source (consumer: validated event-slot geometry drift; main/sub: historical address assertion drift) although current workflow does not execute these historical contracts.

**ROOT CAUSE FOUND FOR THE LAB LOWER BLUE STRIP — regular-BG row index lifetime regression.** Stable master draw_row reconstructs the BG index with `srl t3,s2,1` before `lbu t2,BGXSC(t3)`. Candidate removes that reconstruction and asserts in a comment that t3 remains the real BG index. In fact shared_mirror clobbers t3, and the tile path subsequently sets `t3=CACHE_BASES[BG]+character*(1<<s4)`. finish_row loops directly to draw_row without restoring it. This is a confirmed source/binary defect, not merely the asynchronous-RDP risk. The historical provenance source contract even rejects restoration as a supposedly redundant instruction; that assumption is false once another tile row is drawn.

**Independent exact-evidence match:** original guest byte layout + actual linked RSP .data + source row/address/priority rules predict the captured stripe. BGXSC=0xBAA. For visual phase161, lower section begins s1=7 with valid BG1 map0. Its last character31 leaves t3=0x21F4; next row reads DMEM(BAA+21F4)&FFF=0xD9E, static byte0x4C, selecting map0x9800 of zero entries. Character0 then leaves t3=0x2004; the next row reads DMEM0xBAE, which is WH0=33, selecting false map0x4000 inside BG2 character bytes. Rows with map0x40C0..0x41C0 contain entry0xFF00, whose priority0x2000 causes every tile to be skipped; the intentionally blue backdrop remains visible. Physical rows31..70 are therefore exactly blue. At row71 false-map offset0x4200 is zero and opaque BG1 tile0 draws red again. A host trace independently reproduced all40observed blue rows, and predicts no blue at mixed phase159 (WH0=31 selects zero area0x3800), also matching its actual full framebuffer. This explains a window-dependent intermittent lower stripe with an exact first-hand laboratory match. Native videos do not carry guest phase, so do not claim the exact filmed events have been phase-matched yet; the executed defect is shared by the delivered native runtime.

**SECOND AUDIT FINDING — RDP command-source lifetime is not protected by the existing sender.** src/rsp_main.S:rdp_send (shared regular/Mode7 resident suffix) tests only0x40/CMD_BUSY, writes DP_START in the busy-loop delay slot, then DP_END and returns. Fill/tile callers patch reused DMEM command words before entering this wait; consecutive fill_backdrop segments rewrite RDP_FILL+20/+32/+36. The sender does not establish DPC_CURRENT==previous END or DMA_BUSY0x100 clear before mutable-source reuse or new START. H-COMP SyncFull/0x70 fences are downstream and do not themselves repair earlier command-source reuse. This is a concrete unprotected asynchronous lifetime, not a native trace of a raced command. Nintendo SDK manual distinguishes CMD_BUSY (command buffer full) from DMA_BUSY (DMA in progress); libreultra io/dp.c checks DMA_BUSY before io/dpsetnextbuf.c changes START/END.

**CONFIRMED LAB BLIND SPOT:** exact pinned ares17813a3 n64/rdp/io.cpp always reports DPC DMA_BUSY as0; END write calls flushCommands()->render() synchronously, and bufferBusy clears before it returns. Thus the software command-fetch path cannot expose the source-DMEM reuse race while the submitting RSP continues. Exact pinned vi/io.cpp treats bits10..15 (including pixel-advance12..15) as reserved storage; vi/vi.cpp software scanout does not use pixel-advance. Pinned lab PASS therefore cannot certify either of these native hardware contracts. Vulkan route is conditional and was not promoted to physical timing authority. Native renderer/presentation cause remains OPEN, with this source-lifetime issue now a high-priority match for transient wrong colors, independently of VI pixel-advance.

### Implemented outcome / resource and ownership contract

Complete 256×8 (2048-pixel) color-math output is written into the actual Main framebuffer in the first TM section, before later section state replaces CGWSEL/window bounds. The original eight-row guests map Main to physical framebuffer rows8..15, columns12..267. Declared output scope: original regular BG1/BG2 controls, first section [0,8), full brightness, CGADSUB=0x41 half-add (clipping/absent-selected-Sub may suppress HALF and use saturated full add). Other geometry/operations do not earn a region-output claim; broader historical scalar controls remain input evidence.

The duplicated diagnostic sample body was retired to fit the real loop; its internal pair-loop label was not a dispatch ABI. Frozen externally called entries, loader and shared window/DMA helper binary contracts pass. Main/Mode7 .text=0x1000, H-COMP .text=0x790; fixed loaded-slot boundary/public draw entries preserved, branch-delay hazards=0. Compact rendered TS tags are saved before TM overwrite in the guarded 4480-byte E6000..E717F snapshot, within the audited gap. This deliberately supersedes the old sample-only no-new-per-pixel-surface constraint.

SyncFull fences protect TS/Main readback and now final queue publication: final H-COMP waits for DP TMEM/pipeline/command-buffer idle before queue toggle and RSP HALT. All 22 first-hand capture boundaries (six static + eight visual + eight mixed) have RSP HALT, DPC_CURRENT==DPC_END and all three DP busy flags clear; complete Main/Sub/Main-winner/TS-winner images and both Z guard pairs pass. This is strong pinned-lab ownership evidence, not real-N64 cache/DMA/bus authority.

### Qualified output and genuine mixed work

**VALIDATED / MEASURED in pinned ares:** all 2048 pixels match the independent channel/window reference for fixed HALF, selected-Sub absence/fixed fallback with HALF suppression, present Sub HALF, clipping, prevention, simultaneous clipping+prevention. Local replay of the downloaded actual static/motion captures and branch-delay binaries also PASS.

Visual phases7..14 and mixed phases6..13 each advance by exactly one guest frame; complete supported-region pixels, exact consumed-section phase/provenance and unaffected-region guards pass in all eight frames. Window position and width follow left=n&127, right=left+32+((n>>2)&63).

Mixed guest runs an original IPL-uploaded SPC700 driver with eight looping filter0 BRR voices at declared native-rate pitches500..1375Hz, active timer2, readiness and current CPU-frame echo. Every NMI performs 256 real WRAM read/ALU/write iterations, 512B VRAM DMA and 4B CGRAM DMA while channel0 TS HDMA defines the eight-row section. Actual voice registers/enabled mask, entity results, timer progression, frame echo and nonzero varied stereo PCM all pass. Normal layout ends below that arena; prior dedicated semantic captures used normal, not the delivered HW_PROFILE variant. This is a real defect missed by resource checks, NOT yet a demonstrated explanation of mode0 green/blue/audio symptoms. The exact stable baseline HW_PROFILE ends0xC02B0 but does not use/clear these new candidate CGRAM queues; do not silently patch/relabel stable baseline.

**Stage1 execution plan / current position (consolidated after mixed-load clarification,2026-09-29):** the objective stays first bounded visible composed output. Its delivery scope now also includes a qualified mixed-load guest variant; actual real-N64 stability/cadence acceptance remains stage2. Work already done supplies rendered synthetic Main/Sub, limited winner/source/window arithmetic and eleven semantic controls. **This execution plan is now completed for Stage 1:** the final candidate publishes the full supported band, both moving original guests are qualified, and the exact normal/HW_PROFILE plus matched baseline package is ready. The checklist below preserves the accepted scope; it is not pending work to restart. Actual real-N64 acceptance is Stage 2.

1. Freeze the smallest useful visible workload and expected image/animation, preferably the already-owned eight-line strip with known Main/Sub colors and clipping/prevention inside a moving/resizing window. Reuse the existing reference/control outcomes. This is not the full-screen iris, arbitrary layer coverage or full commercial-game support.
2. Resolve the instruction-space and state/ownership design before extending the code. Only4B loaded-slot padding remains, so a naive appended pixel loop is not viable. Choose minimal refactoring/reuse/retirement of duplicated diagnostic work as needed, preserving necessary semantic evidence, public-entry/loader contracts and memory bounds. Define the correct section-state capture, input lifetime, output destination and RDP->RSP synchronization; do not rely on the old last-section constant-state shortcut for changing windows.
3. Implement computation over the owned region and write actual composed pixels into the frame that is displayed, with appropriate format conversion and bounded DMA/fences. Validate the first visible static pattern before enlarging the supported scope. No diagnostic-record-only result closes this step.
4. Drive deterministic window changes across frames and check that the displayed region follows the corresponding guest state correctly. Compare complete expected output in the supported region and unaffected-region/provenance/ownership guards. Raw-input regression semantics remain; the old post-composition Main-red surface expectation must be explicitly replaced by the correct composed-output oracle, not weakened.
5. Prepare two original diagnostic modes/guests using the same supported output path: a simple visual correctness control and a defined mixed-load variant with real active SPC700/DSP audio, CPU work and relevant bounded DMA/HDMA/PPU activity. Use Road-valid settings and existing measurement infrastructure. It must exercise actual work, not just enable an audio setting; do not claim every unit saturated100% or full-frame graphics capacity. Reuse qualified guest/audio/measurement components where available instead of creating an unrelated stress framework.
6. Run checks appropriate to those changes: actual pixel/reference and animation acceptance, relevant existing semantic controls, supported-geometry/ownership guards, normal/PROFILE builds, branch-delay/ABI checks and laboratory execution. Prepare ready-to-run wrapped .z64 guests, exact runtime/ELF/maps/hashes, fixed settings, expected images/audio and the existing hardware capture/decoding path needed for stage2. CI wall-clock FPS is not hardware/native-cadence authority.
7. Close stage1 only when visible output plus both qualified original test modes and the reproducible hardware package are ready. Stage2 actually runs them on N64 and decides stability/complete-work/native-cadence acceptance for that declared workload. Scope readiness applies to the bounded original guests; stable-master local/private SMW can independently establish a baseline, while candidate commercial readiness, full-frame native cadence and complete iris remain later earned milestones. Record implementation blockers/findings in short batches; do not restart closed sample isolation or require a stand-alone exhaustive window matrix before output work.

**Integrated-load requirement for the early hardware package (user question,2026-09-29):** accompany the visible correctness control with a bounded mixed-load variant exercising the same supported output path alongside defined S-CPU work, active SPC700/DSP sound and relevant DMA/HDMA/PPU updates. Use existing performance capture/decoder infrastructure; do not turn this into a new profiler or exhaustive universal stress project. Baseline versus candidate must use matching guest workload, settings and measurement boundaries.

- Current Main/Sub discriminator is NOT this test: source `make_gate_c_hcomp_main_sub_lifetime.py` waits in WAI between NMIs, initializes graphics and uses TS HDMA, but does not upload/start an active SPC700 sound program. Audio setting enabled and APU running are not proof of an active DSP/music workload. The visible/mixed-load variant still has to be implemented and qualified; do not claim present stress coverage.
- Road-valid NTSC measurement uses frameskip0, APU clock21, audio enabled (retained hardware setting4), precision8 and the existing completed-guest-frame versus native-VI hardware windows (2warmup/5measured60VI windows where appropriate), exact build/ELF/guest/settings and returned SRAM authority. PC/CI wall-clock FPS or an on-screen60 alone is insufficient.
- Assess visible output correctness, complete guest work/presentation, audio activity/stability and required scheduler timing as separate dimensions. No deliberate underclock, muted DSP work, omitted drawing, skips or duplicated frames may manufacture a pass. Preserve established APU timing controls; audible sound or60/60 throughput alone does not prove exhaustive PCM fidelity.
- Full-rate emulation means all required workload work executes at the proper rate; it does not mean every N64/SNES unit must be artificially saturated simultaneously at100%. Mixed load must be defined and stay within the candidate's declared supported geometry/layer/state scope. Arbitrary maximum stress is not a guarantee of commercial-game compatibility or a mandatory60fps claim for every generated worst case.
- An eight-line compositor under mixed CPU/audio/DMA load still measures only that bounded graphics cost. Full-frame evolving windows/sprites/palette states and representative SMW/ALttP/native-cadence acceptance belong to their later scoped stages. Hardware regression on the established Gate-B games is repeated when the candidate safely supports them; do not run an unqualified synthetic-only branch as if it were a general game build.

Operating rules: milestone hardware follows the earliest meaningful candidate, not exhaustive laboratory perfection; no hardware request per tiny commit. Each batch names its emulator blocker/change and minimum necessary evidence. Avoid a stand-alone instrumentation stage, pointless semantic reruns or unrelated architecture expansion. Commercial ROMs stay local/private. Do not treat this list as nine equally sized stages or promise a completion date; later hardware/corpus findings can refine the route without weakening the Road destination. Hardware results must identify exact build/settings/workload/expected outcome, and each important finding is recorded in continuity.

### CONTROLLED GUEST REPAIR — restore WH0 in NMI so line-0 write is inert (2026-09-29 UTC)

- Exact candidate head **`phase4/gate-c-hcomp-transparent-sub-clean@5e84c8095bdce0385a8832cccec47d41521cc9b6`** changes only the transparent-Sub guest generator; Sodium64 semantic runtime remains unchanged.
- Source audit resolved the leading zero-height section from **`0ac4507...`**. Sodium64 **`write_wh0`** compares old/new values and calls **`update_window_frame` only on an actual change**, so repeated WH0=0 writes are inert.
- The prior guest ended each frame with WH0=1 (from the post-line8 HDMA payload) but its NMI still patched the inherited TS restore into **TS=0**. Therefore the next frame's first HDMA transfer changed WH0 **1->0 at line0**, deterministically generating the measured **split=0** record before the intended line8 split.
- The repair keeps startup **TS=0**, but repurposes the inherited same-size NMI instruction slot to **WH0=0**. The startup proof hook still initializes WH0=0 for the first frame. Thus the first per-frame HDMA write of 0 should be a no-op and the only raster-sensitive transition should be WH0 **0->1 at line8**.
- **Expected:** absent queue becomes exactly **`TS=0 split=8 -> TS=0 split=224`** with no leading zero-height record, while raw TS tag **`0x0400`**, presence 0, selected fixed **`0x7C00`**, source/HALF **`0x0002`** and final **`0x7C1F`** remain unchanged. The stale absent-color oracle is intentionally not changed in this batch, so the dedicated workflow may still end red after producing usable evidence.
- **Falsifiers:** split=0 remains; TS becomes nonzero; the line8 bound disappears; present controls or runtime hash change; or semantic mailbox evidence regresses.
- **Static control-flow confirmation:** NMI runs during VBlank; `write_wh0` marks raster state dirty only on the real 1->0 reset, and `vblank_end` then clears **`sect_status`** before **`section_init`** / visible line0. Therefore the NMI reset cannot itself create a visible zero-height section. At visible line0 the HDMA WH0=0 write compares equal and is inert; the first possible visible split is the intended line8 0->1 edge.
- **HYGIENE MEASURED:** exact-head generic **Build and Validate `36580550048` SUCCESS**; normal build, PROFILE build, RSP branch-delay checks and emulator smoke all passed. This guest-only repair does not regress the generic runtime validation surface.

  - force blank is detected later from `STAT_FLAGS&0x80`; after backdrop fill the renderer branches directly to `next_section`, bypassing `next_layer` and therefore bypassing the TS/TM H-COMP traversal whose TM-end path restores baseline `RDP_INIT` OtherModes;
  - next frame's `RDP_FRAME` begins with palette Texture Image + **Load Block (palette/TLUT)**, exactly the operation pinned ares reports as the fatal hardware-bug path.
- This also explains why the rejected cross-frame carrier did **not** crash: its proof state was armed from H-COMP, a path force blank already skips.
- **Classification: SUPPORTED INTERPRETATION, not yet MEASURED cause.** We have not directly sampled RDP OtherModes across the frame boundary.
- **Next controlled repair design:** preserve the exact proof commands/oracle but arm the proof only after the current section is known non-force-blank, and only for section0. Avoid enabling it on startup blank frames; preserve first-section-only TS presence so later section splits cannot overwrite the evidence. Keep public renderer/H-COMP ABI frozen.

### ACTIVE — transparent-Sub fixed-color fallback + HALF suppression discriminator (2026-09-28 UTC)

- **GATE DRIVER:** isolate the remaining second-operand/HALF interaction before moving to color windows. Hardware semantics distinguish **direct fixed-color selection** from **Sub selected but transparent at this pixel**: the latter falls back to fixed color and suppresses HALF.
- Start from exact validated parent **`73d9d08037151457e53c6150c8ebeeb5a176f4ac`**. Preserve the now-validated add/sub/half arithmetic, BG1 winner/eligibility, operands where applicable, compact provenance, section geometry, ABI and one-frame fence.
- First audit the current live-Sub representation to determine whether transparency/coverage survives in the RGBA5551 sample or another already-existing carrier. Do **not** invent a new per-pixel surface until evidence shows the current carrier cannot distinguish a real Sub pixel from transparency.
- Minimal intended discriminator, subject to that carrier audit:
  1. **Direct fixed + HALF** (`CGWSEL bit1=0`) as control: fixed color selected explicitly and HALF remains active.
  2. **Live Sub present + HALF** (`CGWSEL bit1=1`) as the already-validated normal-half control.
  3. **Sub selected but transparent + HALF**: second operand must fall back to fixed color **and HALF must be suppressed** for that pixel.
- Require the source/fallback decision, half-enable decision, Main winner/provenance, section carriers and exact RGB555 result to agree; keep add/sub operation itself fixed so only transparency/fallback semantics vary.
- Falsifiers: no trustworthy transparency carrier exists, present/transparent workloads alter Main/provenance unexpectedly, fallback chooses the wrong operand, HALF is not suppressed only in the transparent-Sub case, fixed-direct control is also suppressed, ABI/guards move, or generic build/smoke regresses.
- **Immediate action:** audit current Sub pixel coverage/transparency evidence and historical clean branches; then design the smallest present-vs-transparent test without importing the old cumulative compositor or beginning window semantics.

| BG2×CG2 | BG2 green `0x03E0` | red `0x001F` | `0x1400 -> mask 0x02` | `0x02` | ON | exact E1f half-add `0x01EF` |

- Exact mailboxes:
  - BG1×CG1: `001F,03E0,01EF,0001,0C00,0001,0001,0000`
  - BG1×CG2: `001F,03E0,001F,0000,0C00,0001,0002,0000`
  - BG2×CG1: `03E0,001F,03E0,0000,1400,0002,0001,0000`
  - BG2×CG2: `03E0,001F,01EF,0001,1400,0002,0002,0000`
- In every state the compact provenance surface is exact: **2,048 active winner-tag words + 192 sentinel border words**, and **both prefix and suffix guards are intact**.
- BG1 states: semantic Main exact **2,048 red `0xF801` + 2,432 sentinel**; Sub exact **2,048 green `0x07C1` + 192 sentinel**.
- BG2 states: semantic Main exact **2,048 green `0x07C1` + 2,432 sentinel**; Sub exact **2,048 red `0xF801` + 192 sentinel**.
- Exactly one physical Main framebuffer is rendered in each independent capture; the other two are untouched. Physical slot identity may rotate between emulator processes and is not semantic identity.
- Both section queues independently carry the requested `TM/TS/CGADSUB/split` matrix.
- Every capture uses one fresh renderer-frame reentry with `guest_frame_delta=1`, **RSP HALT**, **DPC_CURRENT=DPC_END=0x000C58**, and `bufferBusy=false`. Pinned-ares `PIPE_BUSY=true` remains diagnostic only.

### What this proves

- **VALIDATED:** the clean regular-BG renderer can emit first-hand winner identity into a bounded per-pixel provenance carrier.
- **VALIDATED:** BG1 maps to stored winner tag `0x0C00` and native CGADSUB mask `0x01`; BG2 maps to `0x1400` / `0x02`.
- **VALIDATED:** H-COMP can consume that winner identity and gate the already-validated arithmetic path using the correct real CGADSUB bit, not a guest-hardcoded winner.
- **VALIDATED:** the compact provenance lifetime can be bounded to section0 TM and cut before any later-section RDP work.
- **VALIDATED:** the repair does not grow resident IMEM or move the frozen renderer/H-COMP public ABI.

### What this does NOT prove

- Overlapping BG priority arbitration beyond the controlled single-Main-BG workloads.
- BG3/BG4/OBJ/backdrop provenance.
- OBJ palette-group color-math exception.
- `CGWSEL` second-operand selection (Sub vs fixed color), clip/prevent/color-window semantics.
- General add/sub/half mode selection from `CGADSUB` bits; only the already-authoritative E1f half-add operation is exercised here.
- Full-band/full-frame compositor throughput.
- Production-safe real-N64 RDP→RSP ownership/fencing; the proof-time framebuffer/provenance DMA reads are architecture evidence under pinned ares.
- Real-N64 cadence/performance. Emulators remain laboratories, not final authority.

## Rejected explanations / durable negative knowledge

- **REJECTED:** build green implies semantic correctness.
- **REJECTED:** host/emulator full speed implies N64 full speed.
- **REJECTED:** pinned-ares `PIPE_BUSY` must clear for a completed submitted list. In the pinned ares model it remains sticky without Sync Full; completion authority for this lab is RSP HALT + `bufferBusy=false` + `DPC_CURRENT==DPC_END`.
- **REJECTED:** GDB R4300 single-step at the prelaunch fence. It can cross the `jr` delay slot and clear SP HALT. The validated laboratory fence uses the two-breakpoint no-single-step protocol.
- **REJECTED:** semantic Main lives in published framebuffer rows0..7. Validated mapping is rows8..15 for this workload.
- **REJECTED:** physical FB1/FB2/FB3 slot identity is stable across independent ares processes.
- **REJECTED:** a compact Z arena is safe merely because intended color pixels occupy only the compact strip. Z-update lifetime is global RDP state and must be explicitly bounded.
- **REJECTED:** disabling Z in the *next* TS→TM switch is sufficient. Section backdrop work occurs before that switch; the cut must happen at **TM end before next_section work**.
- **REJECTED:** weakening guards or allocating a large full-frame provenance surface to hide the spill. The compact E1c-style ownership model won after correcting lifetime.
- **REJECTED:** importing the old cumulative E2g/E3/E4 compositor as the clean solution. Historical branches are concept/evidence references only unless a specific piece independently earns reintroduction.
- **REJECTED:** the `0ac4507...` all-sentinel absent Sub capture proves the strict blue Sub-surface oracle is wrong. The all-sentinel state was caused by a guest-only zero-height line-0 section; the corrected VBlank WH0 reset restores the exact blue absent Sub surface and passes the unchanged oracle.

## Laboratory / validation boundaries

- Pinned ares: `17813a3ccda21ab9bd45f09bfc2f91196dbf50ff`, N64-only, RSP recompiler forced off for the established proof lab.
- Mupen/LLE smoke is a generic hygiene check, not semantic color-math authority.
- Real N64 remains final authority for cache/TLB, RSP/RDP synchronization, DMA/bus behavior, VI/AI cadence and performance.
- Current proofs are synthetic architecture/measurement proofs. They are not broad software-compatibility claims.
- Do not upload commercial ROMs to the repo. Continue using deterministic/open diagnostic guests until a representative software rung is needed.

## Fixed engineering constraints carried forward

- Preserve frozen renderer/H-COMP overlay addresses unless a deliberate architecture change is separately justified and measured.
- Preserve the validated semantic screen order: low byte TS, high byte TM; shared-screen behavior must not be silently reintroduced from old workarounds.
- Preserve compact Sub ownership and current Main published row mapping as controls while testing color-math semantics.
- Prefer one semantic variable per experiment: `baseline → hypothesis → controlled change → measurement → interpretation → decision`.
- Keep proof-only provenance/fences classified as architecture evidence until a production-safe design wins on real hardware constraints.
- Every candidate must retain normal build + PROFILE build + branch-delay checks; pinned Mupen smoke is generic hygiene, pinned ares dedicated artifacts are experiment authority for these current synthetic discriminators.

## Road-to-1.0 direction

The target is unchanged: N64 real, correct native cadence, one SNES frame per corresponding native frame, frameskip 0, full-rate SPC700/APU and correct audio, high CPU/PPU/DMA/HDMA/timing fidelity, broad compatibility, no per-game manual modes, DSP-1 family, SuperFX/SuperFX2, SA-1, and final hardware validation.

Current Gate-C work is moving that road because it is replacing the inherited renderer's Main/Sub/color-math approximation with measured, bounded semantics. Do not let the proof harness become a second project; each next batch must close a compositor/fidelity uncertainty needed by the Road.

Work is ongoing on v4 completed-boundary Count timings, explicit CPU RSP/VI waits, all-IRQ linked CPU-domain attribution and source-backed RSP stage attribution. Proposed packing remains bounded to the existing 24 KiB recorder budget; compilation and execution qualification are not yet complete. No new hardware FPS improvement or ready diagnostic package is claimed.

The user explicitly rejected duplicated/aliased ROM files. Required workflow: repeatedly load the same personal ROM, arm one capture, reset and reload, preserving multiple automatically identified diagnostic SAV files and the ordinary game save. Current Sodium writes raw cart SRAM; the flashcart menu selects the SD filename/writeback sectors before boot. Investigate a narrowly scoped SummerCart menu integration to reserve ROM-name + diagnostic build + monotonic identifier destinations, importing ordinary progress read-only. Do not ship the discarded ROM-alias preparation approach.

The user also challenged activity-only profiling. The report must identify actual source stages/operations where possible, connect them with frame waits and workload evidence, and distinguish causal confirmation from correlation. Unknown overlay transitions and measurement perturbation must be explicit. One-line scene context is useful but the user need not interpret hardware causes. Keep 20-second capture unless qualification gives a measured reason to change it; avoid per-opcode/per-pixel logging.
